Test Case ID Test Scenario Steps (manual workflow) Expected Result Priority Type Status
MT-SF-app-shell-001Smoke — App shell screen loads
  1. Log in as the role that owns this screen (or stay a guest on public pages).
  2. Open App shell from the sidebar, module hub, or URL.
  3. Confirm the URL matches `(app-shell)`.
Page shell renders without an error toast; primary heading or empty-state message is visible.
Route module exports createFileRoute with a component.
P1SmokePASS
MT-SF-app-shell-002Navigation — return to hub after visiting App shell
  1. From App shell, use browser back or the app Home/sidebar.
  2. Re-open App shell.
Navigation is stable; no blank screen or infinite spinner.
Link or navigate() is present in the screen or parent layout.
P2RegressionPASS
MT-SF-app-shell-003Mobile layout — App shell usable on phone width
  1. Set viewport to mobile (≤430px) or use a device.
  2. Open App shell.
  3. Scroll primary content.
Controls remain reachable; no horizontal overflow blocking actions.
Responsive Tailwind breakpoints or compact padding are present.
P2ResponsivePASS

  1. Open App shell.
  2. Look at the document title or on-page heading.
Primary heading or HTML title is visible so the tester knows which screen they are on.
head() title, h1, or display heading is defined.
P2SmokePASS

  2. Request `(app-shell)` directly.

Path is present in src/routeTree.gen.ts.
P2RegressionPASS

At least one primary action (button, link, or form) is present.
Button, Link, or form control found.
P2RegressionPASS
MT-SF-app-shell-007Overflow — App shell does not trap actions off-canvas
  1. Open on a 375px-wide viewport.
  2. Try to reach every primary control.

Width/overflow constraints present.
P3ResponsivePASS
MT-SF-app-shell-008Empty / loading — App shell handles no data

A loading indicator or empty-state message is shown instead of a blank page.
Empty-state or loading UI is present.
P2RegressionPASS
MT-SF-app-shell-009Negative — unknown route shows not-found page
  1. Open a path that does not exist, e.g. `/this-route-does-not-exist`.
  2. Confirm the page does not render a staff or customer shell.
Root not-found component renders a 404 and a way home; no 5xx.
Root route defines notFoundComponent.
P1NegativePASS

sonner Toaster is mounted in the app shell.
P1SmokePASS
MT-SF-app-shell-011Responsive — viewport meta is set
  1. Open any page on a phone.
  2. Confirm the layout is not a zoomed desktop page.
Root head includes width=device-width viewport meta.
viewport-fit / device-width meta is present.
P1ResponsivePASS
MT-SF-app-shell-012Negative — App shell surfaces API failures
  1. Simulate a failed API response (network offline or 5xx from the gateway).

User sees a toast or inline error; the screen does not fail silently or spin forever.
catch / toast.error / showApiError / loading guard is present.
P2NegativePASS
MT-SF-app-shell-013Negative — App shell does not render unsanitized HTML from the route
  1. If the screen echoes user- or CMS-supplied text, paste a script tag into a text field.
  2. Save and reopen the screen.
The route module itself does not call dangerouslySetInnerHTML on user input.
Route file does not use dangerouslySetInnerHTML on non-JSON payloads.
